# AT24

Offline catch-up and new-node inheritance

Status: pass

Spec: UPD01, UPD02, §13.4, §13.9, §13.10, §13.12

At: 2026-10-30T20:59:15Z

Run: 71f2aa37-b0b9-4568-abe0-2ba7665377e4

Scope: Follow-up 8 requested scenario; not complete original §19 acceptance or physical arm64 proof

Lab: w4-updates, port base 42000

Expected: Accepted 1.0.1 remains current before the later 1.0.2 result; offline batch targets complete consistently with real results; a new node inherits fleet default through its own job without changing the old snapshot

Actual: Offline target caught up through a real success result; a later new node inherited 1.0.2 through its own successful first-sync job; original snapshot unchanged

- policy_quotes: [{"quote":"Heartbeats after acceptance can confirm those\nvalues, but cannot change current/last-good or clear recovery.","source":"docs/updates/central-lifecycle.md:425"},{"quote":"Once a result is accepted, seeding closes even for later jobs in that incarnation.","source":"docs/updates/central-lifecycle.md:506"},{"quote":"A mismatch in\neither release keeps `unconfirmed=true` by withholding that job's observation\ntimestamp; a later agreeing report can confirm it.","source":"docs/updates/central-lifecycle.md:426"},{"quote":"A generation-bearing heartbeat is attributed to the **node's current job only**\n(§13.2, §13.8; D-068, r13 M-1).","source":"docs/updates/central-lifecycle.md:434"},{"quote":"Before that incarnation's first accepted result, pre-result heartbeat seeding\nfills **only a null `nodes.current_release_id`**","source":"docs/updates/central-lifecycle.md:454"},{"quote":"On the current job, each distinct unexpected release/job pair\ngets one `update_observation.unexpected_release` audit","source":"docs/updates/central-lifecycle.md:479"},{"quote":"A\nlater agreeing null last-good permits the issued target to fill the null\n`current_release_id`, without establishing `last_good_release_id`.","source":"docs/updates/central-lifecycle.md:501"}]
